src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  if (!v) return 'available';                 // ⬅️ défaut à "available"

src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:function countAvailSide(zone,row,startNum,width,dir,sel){
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  // dir = -1 (gauche) → on part de startNum-1 ; dir = +1 (droite) → startNum+1
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  let count = 0;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  for (let step = 1; step <= 2; step++) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const n = startNum + dir*step;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const sid = makeSeatId(zone,row,n,width);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (!SEAT_STATUS.has(sid)) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      // au bord de la tribune : on considère comme ">= 2" pour éviter tout blocage
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      return 2;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (isAvailableSeat(sid, sel)) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      count++;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    } else {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      break;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  return count; // 0, 1 ou 2 (2 = "au moins 2")
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:}

src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:function checkLocalNoSingleGap(items){
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  const sel = buildSelectedSet(items);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  if (!sel.size) return null;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  for (const sid of sel) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const p = parseSeatId(sid); if (!p) continue;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // On ne traite que le siège "bord gauche" d'un bloc sélectionné pour éviter les doublons
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const L1 = makeSeatId(p.zone,p.row,p.num-1,p.width);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (sel.has(L1)) continue; // pas le bord gauche du bloc → on passe
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // Trouver l’extrémité droite du bloc (bord droit)
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    let rightNum = p.num;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    while (sel.has(makeSeatId(p.zone,p.row,rightNum+1,p.width))) rightNum++;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const leftNum = p.num; // on est bien le bord gauche du bloc
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const blockLen = (rightNum - leftNum + 1);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // Comptes de libres contigus à gauche/droite (bornés à 2 ; bord = 2)
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const leftAvail  = countAvailSide(p.zone,p.row,leftNum, p.width, -1, sel);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const rightAvail = countAvailSide(p.zone,p.row,rightNum,p.width, +1, sel);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:if (DEBUG) dlog('gapCheck', { row:p.row, zone:p.zone, blockLen, leftAvail, rightAvail, leftNum, rightNum });
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // Règle (STRICT) :
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    //  - bloc = 1  → interdit si left==1 ou right==1
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    //  - bloc ≥ 2  → interdit si left==1 OU right==1  (même un seul côté)
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (blockLen <= 1) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (leftAvail === 1)  return { zone:p.zone, row:p.row, side:'left',  seat:sid };
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (rightAvail === 1) return { zone:p.zone, row:p.row, side:'right', seat:sid };
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    } else {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (leftAvail === 1 && rightAvail === 1) return { zone:p.zone, row:p.row, side:'both',  seat:sid };
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (leftAvail === 1 && rightAvail > 0)   return { zone:p.zone, row:p.row, side:'left',  seat:sid };
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (rightAvail === 1 && leftAvail > 0)    return { zone:p.zone, row:p.row, side:'right', seat:sid };
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  return null;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:}

src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:function extractHaMessages(from) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  try {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // Cas 1 : objet JSON déjà parsé
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (from && typeof from === 'object') {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (Array.isArray(from.errors)) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:        return from.errors.map(e => e?.message || e?.code || 'Champ invalide');
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (from.error && typeof from.error === 'object' && Array.isArray(from.error.errors)) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:        return from.error.errors.map(e => e?.message || e?.code || 'Champ invalide');
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      // Certains backends encapsulent encore sous .error.message (string JSON)
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      if (typeof from.error === 'string') {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:        return extractHaMessages(from.error);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // Cas 2 : chaîne brute (ex: "HelloAsso checkout 400 {\"errors\":[{...}]}")
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const text = typeof from === 'string' ? from : '';
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (!text) return [];
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // a) si la chaîne entière est du JSON
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (text.trim().startsWith('{') || text.trim().startsWith('[')) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      try {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:        const j = JSON.parse(text);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:        return extractHaMessages(j);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      } catch {/* on continue */}
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // b) extraire toutes les valeurs "message":"...".
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const msgs = [];
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    const re = /"message"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/g;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    let m;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    while ((m = re.exec(text))) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      msgs.push(m[1]);
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (msgs.length) return msgs;
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    // c) fallback : si on voit "HelloAsso checkout 400", message générique
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    if (/helloasso\s+checkout\s+400/i.test(text)) {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:      return ['Certaines informations ne sont pas valides. Veuillez corriger les champs en erreur.'];
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    return [];
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  } catch {
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:    return [];
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:  }
src/public/static/js/generic-view.js:}

src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  function makeHeaders() {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const h = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const raw = loadAuthHeader().trim();
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    if (!raw) return h;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    if (raw.includes(':')) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const i = raw.indexOf(':');
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const k = raw.slice(0, i).trim();
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const v = raw.slice(i + 1).trim();
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      if (k && v) h[k] = v;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    } else {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      h['Authorization'] = raw; // ex: "Bearer xxxxx"
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    return h;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  async function fetchJson(url, opts = {}) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const res = await fetch(url, { ...opts, headers: { ...makeHeaders(), ...(opts.headers || {}) } });
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    return res.json();
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  }

src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  }

src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    if (!lines.length) return [];
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const headers = splitCsvLine(lines[0]).map(h => h.trim());
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const rows = [];
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const cells = splitCsvLine(lines[i]);
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const obj = {};
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      headers.forEach((h, idx) => obj[h] = cells[idx] ?? '');
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      rows.push(obj);
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    return rows;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  function splitCsvLine(line) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    const out = [];
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    let cur = '', q = false;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    for (let i=0; i<line.length; i++) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      const c = line[i];
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      if (q) {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        if (c === '"' && line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        else if (c === '"') { q = false; }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        else cur += c;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      } else {
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        if (c === '"') q = true;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        else if (c === ',') { out.push(cur); cur = ''; }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:        else cur += c;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:      }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    }
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    out.push(cur);
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:    return out;
src/views/admin/admin-pricing.js:  }

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:export async function findSingleGaps({ seasonCode, venueSlug, selectedSeatIds }) {

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:  // Groupe la sélection par (zone|row) pour ne requêter que les rangées concernées

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:  const problems = [];
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:  for (const [key, picks] of byZR.entries()) {
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    const [zoneKey, row] = key.split('|');
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    // Charge TOUS les sièges existants de cette zone/rangée (peut être partiel / trous)

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    if (!nodes.length) continue; // rien à valider
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    // Marque la sélection courante comme "occupée après sélection"

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    // Parcourt la ligne et construit des segments d'**indisponibilité** numérique:

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    // de deux sièges occupés contigus (numériquement).
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    let segStart = -1; // début d'un segment d'AVAIL (indices)
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:    for (let i = 0; i <= nodes.length; i++) {

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      const curAvail = cur && !cur.occupied;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      const numberingGap = !!(cur && prev && cur.num !== prev.num + 1);
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      // Si on a un trou de numérotation, on flush le segment précédent car c'est une frontière
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      const mustFlushForGap = numberingGap && segStart !== -1;

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      if (mustFlushForGap || mustFlushForEnd) {

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          const leftIdx  = segStart - 1;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          const rightIdx = segEnd + 1;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          const here     = nodes[segStart];
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          const leftOK   = leftIdx >= 0
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:                           && nodes[leftIdx].occupied
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:                           && nodes[leftIdx].num === here.num - 1;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          const rightOK  = rightIdx < nodes.length
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:                           && nodes[rightIdx].occupied
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:                           && nodes[rightIdx].num === here.num + 1;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          if (leftOK && rightOK) {

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:          }
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:        }
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:        segStart = -1;
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      }
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:
src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      // Démarre un nouveau segment après un gap ou après un siège occupé

src/utils/no-single-gap.js:      }

src/utils/money.js:export function splitInstallments(totalCents, count, firstDue = new Date()) {
src/utils/money.js:  if (![1,2,3].includes(count)) throw new Error('count must be 1|2|3');
src/utils/money.js:  const base = Math.floor(totalCents / count);
src/utils/money.js:  const remainder = totalCents - base * count;
src/utils/money.js:  const schedule = [];
src/utils/money.js:  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
src/utils/money.js:    const amount = base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0);
src/utils/money.js:    const dueDate = new Date(firstDue);
src/utils/money.js:    if (i > 0) dueDate.setMonth(dueDate.getMonth() + i);
src/utils/money.js:    schedule.push({ dueDate, amountCents: amount, status: 'pending' });
src/utils/money.js:  }
src/utils/money.js:  return schedule;
src/utils/money.js:}
src/utils/money.js:
src/utils/money.js:/** Version "montants seuls" (pour HelloAsso terms[]) */
src/utils/money.js:export function splitInstallmentAmounts(totalCents, count) {
src/utils/money.js:  if (![1,2,3].includes(count)) throw new Error('count must be 1|2|3');
src/utils/money.js:  const base = Math.floor(totalCents / count);
src/utils/money.js:  const remainder = totalCents - base * count;
src/utils/money.js:  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0));
src/utils/money.js:}

src/routes/ha.js:function normalizeHaStatus(input, fallback) {
src/routes/ha.js:  // déballage d'objets potentiels
src/routes/ha.js:  let raw = input;
src/routes/ha.js:  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
src/routes/ha.js:    raw =
src/routes/ha.js:      raw.status || raw.state || raw.code || raw.result || raw.paymentStatus ||
src/routes/ha.js:      (raw.data && (raw.data.status || raw.data.state || raw.data.code)) || '';
src/routes/ha.js:  }
src/routes/ha.js:  raw = String(raw || fallback || '').trim().toLowerCase();
src/routes/ha.js:  if (!raw) return '';
src/routes/ha.js:  // mapping simple vers une poignée de valeurs canoniques
src/routes/ha.js:  if (raw === 'payment_succeeded' || raw === 'success' || raw === 'succeeded' || raw === 'ok') return 'succeeded';
src/routes/ha.js:  if (raw === 'paid' || raw === 'payment_accepted' || raw === 'processed') return 'paid';
src/routes/ha.js:  if (raw.startsWith('authoriz')) return 'authorized';
src/routes/ha.js:  return raw;
src/routes/ha.js:}

scripts/import-subscribers.js:// Petit parseur CSV qui gère les champs entre guillemets et les virgules internes.
scripts/import-subscribers.js:function parseCsvText(text) {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  const lines = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter(l => l.trim() !== '');
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  if (lines.length === 0) return { headers: [], rows: [] };
scripts/import-subscribers.js:
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  const parseLine = (line) => {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    const out = [];
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    let cur = '';
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    let inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:      const ch = line[i];
scripts/import-subscribers.js:      if (inQuotes) {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        if (ch === '"') {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          if (i + 1 < line.length && line[i + 1] === '"') {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:            cur += '"'; i++; // échappe ""
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          } else {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:            inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          }
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        } else {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        }
scripts/import-subscribers.js:      } else {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        if (ch === '"') {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          inQuotes = true;
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        } else if (ch === ',') {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          out.push(cur);
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          cur = '';
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        } else {
scripts/import-subscribers.js:          cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers.js:        }
scripts/import-subscribers.js:      }
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    }
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    out.push(cur);
scripts/import-subscribers.js:    return out.map(s => s.trim());
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  };
scripts/import-subscribers.js:
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  const headers = parseLine(lines[0]).map(h => h.trim());
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  const rows = lines.slice(1).map(parseLine);
scripts/import-subscribers.js:  return { headers, rows };

scripts/reports/import-orders.js:// - --keepIds     : conserve l'ObjectId d'origine (colonne orderId du CSV). Si déjà existant, skip ou --overwrite.

scripts/reports/import-orders.js:function parseCsvLine(line) {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  // Parse CSV simple avec guillemets/échappement "..."
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  const out = [];
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  let cur = '';
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  let i = 0;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  let inQuotes = false;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  while (i < line.length) {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:    if (inQuotes) {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      if (ch === '"') {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:        if (line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i += 2; continue; } // ""
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:        inQuotes = false; i++; continue;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      } else {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:        cur += ch; i++; continue;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      }
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:    } else {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      if (ch === '"') { inQuotes = true; i++; continue; }
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      if (ch === ',') { out.push(cur); cur = ''; i++; continue; }
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:      cur += ch; i++;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:    }
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  }
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  out.push(cur);
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  return out;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:}
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:

scripts/reports/import-orders.js:function extractHex24(s) {
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  if (!s) return null;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  const str = String(s).trim();
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  // Supporte: 24-hex nu, ObjectId("..."), ou une chaîne contenant quelque part un 24-hex

scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  if (direct) return direct;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  const m = str.match(/[a-fA-F0-9]{24}/);
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:  return m ? m[0] : null;
scripts/reports/import-orders.js:}

scripts/reports/import-provisions.js://
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// Logique de statut :
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// - Si --useCsvStatus ET status CSV non vide => on l'applique tel quel.
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// - Sinon, si tag == UNAVAILABLE => status = 'unavailable'.
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// - Sinon, status = --provisionStatus (défaut 'busy').
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// - On ne modifie jamais les sièges 'booked' (sécurité).
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js://
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// --clearFirst : remet à 'available' tous les sièges provisoires (busy/provisioned/blocked)
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js://                de la saison/lieu (booked intouché, 'unavailable' conservé sauf --clearUnavailable).
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js://
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// --onlyIfAvailable : n'applique le statut que si le siège est actuellement 'available'.
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js://
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:// --dryRun : n'écrit pas en base.

scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:function parseCsvLine(line) {
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  const out = []; let cur = ''; let i = 0; let inQ = false;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  while (i < line.length) {
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:    if (inQ) {
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      if (ch === '"') {
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:        if (line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i += 2; continue; }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:        inQ = false; i++; continue;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      cur += ch; i++; continue;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:    } else {
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      if (ch === '"') { inQ = true; i++; continue; }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      if (ch === ',') { out.push(cur); cur=''; i++; continue; }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:      cur += ch; i++;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:    }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  }
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  out.push(cur);
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  return out;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:}

scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  const parts = s.split('-');
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  if (parts.length < 3) return s;
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  const last = parts.pop();
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  if (/^\d+$/.test(last)) parts.push(last.padStart(3, '0')); else parts.push(last);
scripts/reports/import-provisions.js:  return parts.join('-');

scripts/reports/export-provision.js:function padSeatNum(seatId) {
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  const s = String(seatId || '');
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  const parts = s.split('-');
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  if (parts.length < 3) return s;
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  const last = parts.pop();
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  if (/^\d+$/.test(last)) {
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:    parts.push(last.padStart(3, '0'));
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:    return parts.join('-');
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  }
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  return s;
scripts/reports/export-provision.js:}

scripts/reports/export-provision.js:    rowKey:  parts[1] || '',

scripts/reports/export-provision.js:  // VISITORS

scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:function detectDelimiter(line) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let comma = 0, semi = 0, inQ = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  for (let i=0;i<line.length;i++) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    if (ch === '"') inQ = !inQ;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    else if (!inQ) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === ',') comma++;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else if (ch === ';') semi++;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  return semi > comma ? ';' : ','; // défaut: virgule
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:}

scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:function parseCSVLine(line, delim) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  const out = [];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let cur = '';
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  for (let i=0; i<line.length; i++) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    if (inQuotes) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === '"') {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        if (line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        else inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      } else {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    } else {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === '"') inQuotes = true;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else if (ch === delim) { out.push(cur); cur = ''; }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  out.push(cur);
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  return out.map(s => s.trim());
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:}

scripts/docs/build-docs.js:
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<h2>1. Pré-requis</h2>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<ul>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:  <li>Ubuntu 22.04, Node ≥ 20, npm ≥ 10, MongoDB, Nginx, Certbot.</li>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:  <li>DNS : <code>billetterie-dev.belougas.fr</code> (INT) et <code>billetterie.belougas.fr</code> (PROD) → IP VPS.</li>

scripts/docs/build-docs.js:
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<h2>2. Vérifications de base</h2>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<pre><code>node -v           # → v20.x
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:npm -v            # → 10.x
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:mongod --version  # → &gt;= 6.x
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:nginx -v          # → nginx/1.2x
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:</code></pre>

scripts/docs/build-docs.js:// PROD
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:use bts_prod
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:db.createUser({ user:"bts_prod", pwd:"***", roles:[{role:"readWrite",db:"bts_prod"}] })
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:</code></pre>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<p><em>Tests attendus</em> :</p>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:<pre><code>mongosh "mongodb://bts_int:&lt;PASS&gt;@127.0.0.1:27017/bts_int?authSource=bts_int" --eval 'db.runCommand({ping:1})'
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:# → { ok: 1 }
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:</code></pre>
scripts/docs/build-docs.js:

scripts/provision-seats.js:// Construit un RegExp qui matche un seatId où la dernière partie numérique accepte 0..n zéros en tête.
scripts/provision-seats.js:// ex: patternForId('S5-F-048') => /^S5-F-0*48$/  (matche S5-F-048, S5-F-48)
scripts/provision-seats.js:function patternForId(id) {
scripts/provision-seats.js:  const s = String(id || '');
scripts/provision-seats.js:  const parts = s.split('-');
scripts/provision-seats.js:  const last = parts.pop() || '';
scripts/provision-seats.js:  if (!/^\d+$/.test(last)) return new RegExp('^' + s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + '$');
scripts/provision-seats.js:  const prefix = parts.join('-').replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
scripts/provision-seats.js:  const num = String(parseInt(last, 10)); // dépouille les zéros en tête
scripts/provision-seats.js:  return new RegExp(`^${prefix}-0*${num}$`);
scripts/provision-seats.js:}
